Akron play home games at Kryľja Sovetov's home stadium in Samara (100 km south-east of Togliatti). 
  The club has put its own stadium project on hold, while the existing Togliatti stadium needs 
  significant renovation.
Dinámo (Mahačkalá) play home games in Kaspíjsk (the satellite city south-east of Mahačkalá).

Dinámo (Mahačkalá) play home games in Kaspíjsk (the satellite city south-east of Mahačkalá).

Second League - Division A

Golden Group | Silver Group

Golden Group

Promotion Format:
- Top-2 Second Stage teams are directly promoted to the First League 2025/26.
- The best of the teams placed 4 to 6 after the Second Stage that has taken 
  1st to 3rd place at the First Stage enters the promotion playoff vs. the team
  placed 3rd at the Second Stage.
- If all the teams placed 4 to 6 after the Second Stage have been placed 4th or
  lower at the First Stage, the Second Stage third-placed team is promoted to
  the First League 2025/26.

Relegation Format:
- After both First and Second Stage, bottom-4 teams are relegated to the Silver Group.

PROMOTION PLAYOFF [Jun 24, 28]

NB: Mašúk-CMW who have earned the right to play FC Čeljábinsk for the third spot in First League
    failed to obtain the First League license, thus giving the right to Volgáŕ.

Volgáŕ (Ástrahań)  1-1 0-0  FC Čeljábinsk  [agg 1-1, pen 2-4]
  [1. Artjóm Pogósov 47 - Alexándr Nósov 18. Att: 10,725]
  [2. Att: 5,078]

FC Čeljábinsk promoted together with Sparták (Kostromá) and Vólga (Uľjánovsk).

Silver Group

Promotion Format:
- After both First and Second Stages, top-4 teams are promoted to the Golden Group.

Relegation Format:
- After the First Stage, bottom-2 teams are relegated to Division B 2025.
- If one or two teams placed 9-10 after the Second Stage of Division A 
  Silver Group 2023/24 season takes 5th-6th place at the First Stage,
  they enter the relegation playoff vs. teams placed 7th-8th.
  In case of two teams, Place 5 plays Place 8 and Place 6 plays Place 7;
  In case of one team, it plays Place 7, while the team finished 8th is
  directly relegated;
  In case of no teams, the teams finished 7th and 8th are directly relegated.
- After the Second Stage, there is no relegation with the bottom-2 teams 
  qualifying for the next season relegation playoff.
